TL;DR: 本文研究了基于人物传记的对话系统中，传记可见性在训练、推理和评估三个阶段分别设置（而非作为单一因素）时对系统行为的影响。研究发现训练阶段的可视性比推理阶段的更能决定模型是通过对话表达人物特质还是直接复制传记文本；训练时可见对话者传记会减少目标传记文本的复制；在不对称披露（仅对话者看到目标传记）时，目标内容更容易泄露到对话者轮次中，法官更容易识别这些对话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的人物对话系统研究通常将训练、推理和评估阶段的传记可见性视为一个统一因素，忽略了对话中至少有两方参与者，且谁可以看到谁的传记在不同阶段可能不同。这种简化掩盖了仅在分开调节三个阶段可见性时才显现的机制。

Method: 在配对说话者传记的对话数据集上进行系统实验，分别改变训练和推理阶段中目标说话者和对话者是否能看到对方的传记（三阶段分解，但评估阶段通过LLM判断进行作者识别），使用LLM作为法官进行作者身份识别。

Result: (i) 训练阶段的可视性比推理阶段更能决定模型是通过对话表现人物特质还是退化为复制传记文本；(ii) 训练时使用对话者传记可见的模型比不用该设置的模型复制更少的目标传记文本，而仅在推理阶段改变可见性效果不一致；(iii) 在不对称披露（只有对话者看到目标传记）下，目标内容更频繁地泄露到对话者轮次中，含有此类痕迹的对话更容易被法官正确识别，尤其是当对话者轮次可见时。

Conclusion: 传记向生成轮次的泄露是对话者可见性在训练和推理阶段配置方式所导致的产物，将训练、推理和评估三阶段的传记可见性分开考虑是必要且重要的。

Abstract: Persona-based dialogue systems are usually conditioned on speaker biography, but dialogues involve at least two participants, and who has access to whose biography can vary across training, inference, and evaluation. Prior work often neglected these aspects, obscuring mechanisms that only appear when biography visibility is toggled separately across training, inference, and evaluation, a three-stage factorisation that prior work has largely treated as a single factor. We study this factorisation on a dataset of dialogues paired with speaker's biographies, varying whether the target and interlocutor speakers see each other's biographies during training and inference, and using an LLM as a judge to perform author identification. We find that (i) training-time visibility, more than inference-time visibility, determines whether models express persona traits through dialogue or fall back on copying biographical text (a known problem/phenomenon in persona-based generation); (ii) models trained with interlocutor-biography visibility copy less target-biographical text than models trained without it, while changing visibility only at inference time has a less consistent effect; and (iii) under asymmetric disclosure, where only the interlocutor sees the target biography, target content leaks into interlocutor turns more often, and dialogues containing such traces are easier for the judge to identify, especially when interlocutor turns are visible. These results suggest that biography leakage into generated turns is an artefact of how interlocutor visibility is configured across training and inference, and separating the three stages is necessary.

</details>


### [128] [ContextPilot: Teaching Agents for Proactive Context Management via Fine-grained RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28476)
*Zhuoshi Pan,Qizhi Pei,Junru Lu,Honglin Lin,H. Vicky Zhao,Di Yin,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ContextPilot是一个面向长时程agentic推理的主动上下文管理框架，通过扩充规划、长期记忆和软上下文卸载工具，并设计针对上下文管理定制的强化学习方法（利用上下文与熵变化识别关键编辑决策、从分支轨迹中估计动作级优势），在长上下文QA和深度搜索任务上以更紧凑的工作上下文取得更强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程agentic任务需要LLM在多轮交互中迭代检索、整合和维护分散信息，但保留全部交互历史会导致工作上下文持续膨胀。现有主动上下文管理方法存在三大局限：工具集受限（仅支持搜索、删除和摘要，缺乏全局规划、长期记忆与自适应压缩）；探索效率低下（对所有上下文管理动作一视同仁，忽略其对最终结果的不同影响）；信用分配粒度粗糙（RL中将最终轨迹级奖励分配给所有中间上下文编辑动作）。

Method: ContextPilot从两方面入手：(1) 系统性地扩充工具集，新增规划（planning）、长期记忆（long-term memory）和软上下文卸载（soft context offloading）工具；(2) 提出针对上下文管理定制的RL方法，利用上下文与熵的变化识别关键编辑决策以进行分支采样，并从所有经过对应上下文编辑动作的分支轨迹中估计动作级优势，从而实现细粒度的信用分配。

Result: 在长上下文问答和深度搜索任务上的实验表明，ContextPilot在更紧凑的工作上下文下取得了更强的性能，并在多种基础模型和基准测试上一致优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: ContextPilot通过扩充上下文管理工具集和设计定制的强化学习信用分配机制，有效解决了现有主动上下文管理中工具受限、探索低效和信用分配粗糙的问题，验证了在长时程agentic推理中主动上下文管理的潜力。代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic tasks require large language models (LLMs) to iteratively retrieve, integrate, and maintain dispersed information across multi-turn interactions, but preserving all interaction histories leads to a continuously growing working context. Recent proactive context management methods allow models to edit their own working context with specialized tools, yet they still face three key limitations: (1) a limited toolset restricted to search, deletion, and summarization, with no support for global planning, long-term memory, and adaptive compression; (2) inefficient exploration that treats context management actions uniformly despite their heterogeneous impacts on final outcomes; and (3) coarse-grained credit assignment that assigns the final trajectory-level reward to all intermediate context editing actions during RL. To bridge these gaps, we introduce ContextPilot, a proactive context management framework for long-horizon agentic reasoning. Our approach systematically augments the toolset with planning, long-term memory, and soft context offloading tools. We further propose an RL method tailored for context management, which uses context and entropy variation to identify critical editing decisions for branch sampling and estimates action-level advantages from all branched trajectories that pass through the corresponding context editing action. Experiments on long-context QA and deep search tasks show that ContextPilot achieves stronger performance with a more compact working context, consistently outperforming existing baselines across various base models and benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/Tencent/ContextPilot.

</details>


### [129] [Blind Men and the Elephant: Probing the Epistemic Myopia of LLMs under Long-Tail Divergent Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28478)
*Zhuoshi Pan,Junru Lu,Yan Qian,H. Vicky Zhao,Di Yin,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ElephantBench 是一个用于评估大语言模型对长尾事实多账户记忆能力的新型闭卷知识探针，包含 1,094 个通过可审计图流水线生成的问题。实验发现即使最强的模型也仅在 52.4% 的问题上能同时回忆起两个对立账户，模型规模和推理时间扩展只能部分改善但不能消除这种不完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的问答评估假设每个事实只有一个标准答案，忽略了LLM可能对长尾事实保留相互矛盾的多种版本。研究者旨在揭示模型参数化记忆中的"认识论近视"（epistemic myopia），即模型能否保留对同一事实的不同叙述。

Method: 构建了一个基于图的流水线：从低曝光网络语料库中检索相关文档，识别自然产生的分歧（对同一事实的不同叙述），将其转换为多账户问答记录。每个答案都经过原始文档和权威公开网络来源的双重验证，并由人类标注者审阅。最终生成包含1,094个问题的ElephantBench数据集。评估了32个模型，并进行了语料库暴露分析。

Result: 最强模型仅能在52.4%的问题上恢复两个账户，其余几乎所有问题都只能回忆一个账户而遗漏另一个。模型规模扩大和推理时推理能力提升可以改善回忆率，但不能完全消除不完整性。语料分析显示，暴露不均衡偏向主导账户，而少数方更高的暴露与更完整的回忆相关。

Conclusion: ElephantBench作为可复现的知识探针，为诊断LLM参数化记忆中的认识论近视提供了有效工具。图构建流水线为将长尾语料转化为可溯源的知识探针提供了高效可扩展的途径，有助于评估和推进下一代LLM的认识论严谨性。

Abstract: Factual question answering (QA) typically assumes a single canonical answer, obscuring whether large language models (LLMs) retain divergent accounts of long-tail facts. To address this gap, we introduce ElephantBench, a closed-book knowledge probe comprising 1,094 questions generated through an auditable graph-based pipeline. The pipeline retrieves related documents from a low-exposure web corpus, identifies naturally occurring disagreements, and converts them into multi-account QA records. Each answer is verified against the originating documents and authoritative public web sources and is then reviewed by human annotators. Across 32 models, even the strongest model recovers both accounts on only 52.4% of questions, while on nearly all remaining questions it recalls one account but omits the other. Scaling model size and inference-time reasoning improve recall but do not eliminate this incompleteness. Corpus analysis further shows that exposure imbalance favors the dominant account, whereas greater minority-side exposure is associated with more complete recall. These findings establish ElephantBench as a reproducible knowledge probe for diagnosing epistemic myopia in parametric memory. More broadly, our graph-based benchmark construction pipeline provides an efficient and scalable way to turn long-tail corpora into source-traceable knowledge probes, supporting efforts to evaluate and advance the epistemic rigour of next-generation LLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/Tencent/ElephantBench.

</details>


### [130] [NL2AGBench: Benchmarking LLM Auto-Formalization for AlphaGeometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28481)
*Samuel Xiao,Judy Song,Rory Hu,Ziliang Zong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了自然语言到AlphaGeometry基准（NL2AGBench），用于评估大语言模型将英文几何问题翻译为AlphaGeometry形式化表述的能力。基准采用基于执行的验证方式评估翻译质量，实验揭示了闭源与开源模型之间的显著性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在自然语言理解和数学推理方面表现优异，但其将非正式数学问题转化为形式化表示的能力尚未被充分探索。对于AlphaGeometry这类神经符号几何系统，手动将自然语言问题转化为其专用DSL语法是重要的可用性瓶颈，亟需一个标准化的基准来评估和提升LLM在此任务上的表现。

Method: 构建NL2AGBench基准，使用执行式验证（在AlphaGeometry内运行翻译结果）而非文本相似度来评估翻译质量；评估十个覆盖不同参数规模的闭源和开源LLM；分析可执行翻译准确率、语法正确性和错误特征；提出错误分类法区分语法错误和逻辑错误；探索少样本提示、微调和人指导提示等缓解策略。

Result: 闭源与开源模型之间存在显著性能差距：领先的闭源模型可执行翻译率超过80%，而即使最大的开源模型也难以一致地保持几何约束并生成有效的形式化表述。所提出的缓解策略（少样本提示、微调、人指导提示）在多个模型族上带来了可测量的改进。

Conclusion: NL2AGBench揭示了LLM在几何问题形式化翻译任务中的现状与差距，验证了执行式评估的有效性，并为提升LLM几何形式化能力和改进神经符号几何系统的可用性提供了方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in natural language understanding and mathematical reasoning. However, their ability to translate informal mathematical problems into formal representations remains underexplored. This limitation is particularly important for neuro-symbolic geometry systems such as AlphaGeometry, whose theorem-proving engine requires inputs in a specialized domain-specific language (DSL). Although AlphaGeometry achieves near-IMO gold-medalist performance, manually converting natural-language problems into its formal syntax remains a significant usability bottleneck. To address this challenge, we introduce the Natural Language to AlphaGeometry Benchmark (NL2AGBench), which evaluates LLMs in translating English geometry problems into AlphaGeometry-compatible formal representations. NL2AGBench uses execution-based verification within AlphaGeometry to assess translation quality rather than relying solely on textual similarity. We evaluate ten state-of-the-art open- and closed-source LLMs across multiple parameter scales and analyze executable translation accuracy, syntactic correctness, and error characteristics. Our experiments reveal a substantial performance gap between closed- and open-source models: leading closed-source models achieve executable translation rates above 80%, while even the largest open-source models struggle to consistently preserve geometric constraints and produce valid formalizations. We introduce an error taxonomy distinguishing syntax and logic errors and investigate mitigation strategies, including few-shot prompting, fine-tuning, and human-guided hinting, which yield measurable improvements across multiple model families.

</details>


### [131] [Ladders in Chaos: When, How, (and Perhaps Why) Does Test-Time Scaling Improve LLM Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28496)
*Di Wu,Sergey Troshin,Christof Monz,Antske Fokkens,Vlad Niculae*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究系统比较了大型语言模型在机器翻译任务中两种测试时扩展范式（序贯采样与并行采样）的差异，发现序贯采样（后续回答依赖先前尝试）具有更高的性能天花板，尤其是在较小的采样预算下能生成更多样化、更有效的候选样本。通过多维度人工分析发现，序贯采样能显著提升翻译的流畅性和自然度，但在推理预算较大时可能损害准确性。机制分析表明，序贯扩展的成功部分归因于模型能够访问更大的目标侧上下文，消融实验显示序贯采样对采样温度具有鲁棒性，但对上下文构建方式较为敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展已成为提升LLM性能的两种主流范式（序贯和并行），但这两者在机器翻译任务中的性质差异尚未被系统研究。作者旨在深入理解序贯采样为何及如何在翻译中表现更优，以及其潜在的机制和局限性，为测试时扩展策略的选择和优化提供理论依据。

Method: 本研究采用混合方法：（1）对比实验：系统比较序贯采样和并行采样（i.i.d.采样加重排序）在翻译任务中的性能表现；（2）多维度人工分析：对Best-of-N翻译结果进行流畅性、自然度和准确性等多维度的人工评估；（3）机制探析：通过受控分析将序贯自改进的成功部分归因于更大的目标侧上下文；（4）消融实验：测试不同采样温度下的鲁棒性以及不同上下文构建方式的敏感性。

Result: 研究结果表明：序贯采样具有更高的性能上限，在较小采样预算下表现尤为突出；序贯采样显著改善翻译的流畅性和自然度，但在推理预算增大时会降低准确性；序贯扩展的成功机制部分源于模型对更大目标侧上下文的访问；序贯采样对温度参数具有鲁棒性，但对上下文构建方式敏感。

Conclusion: 序贯测试时扩展在机器翻译中是优于并行扩展的范式，尤其是在资源受限场景下。然而其在不同上下文构建方式下的敏感性表明，该领域仍有优化空间，未来工作可聚焦于改进上下文构建策略以进一步提升序贯采样的翻译质量与稳定性。

Abstract: Two forms of test-time scaling for Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as effective and widely adopted paradigms: sequential, in which later answer attempts depend on earlier ones, and parallel, such as i.i.d. sampling with reranking. In this study, we investigate their properties in translation. First, our study shows that sequential sampling has a higher performance ceiling, providing a more diverse and effective pool of samples, particularly under smaller sampling budgets. Second, we interrogate the nature of test-time scaling through a multidimensional manual analysis. Human analysis of the Best-of-$N$ translations demonstrates that sequential sampling substantially improves translation fluency and naturalness, but can degrade accuracy when inference budgets are large. Finally, we suggest an explanation of the mechanism through which sequential scaling improves machine translation. Our controlled analysis partially attributes the success of sequential self-improvement to the model's access to a larger target-side context. Ablation experiments on sequential sampling demonstrate its robustness across different sampling temperatures, while also revealing sensitivity to context construction, suggesting directions for future improvement.

</details>


### [132] [Phoneme- and Word-Level Metrics Using Self-Supervised Speech Representations for Forced Alignment Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28508)
*V. S. D. S. Mahesh Akavarapu,Michael Daniel,Gerhard Jäger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于自监督语音表示的无参考强制对齐评估指标——音素-聚类互信息（PCMI）和单词声学一致性分数（WACS），可在无需人工标注时间戳的情况下大规模评估强制对齐质量，并在85种语言的FLEURS、45种语言的DoReCo以及两种音系复杂的低资源语言上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的强制对齐评估需要人工标注时间戳，这不仅成本高昂，还限制了大规模和多语言场景下的分析。作者旨在引入无需参考标注的语料级评估指标，以突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出两种基于自监督语音表示的语料级指标：(1) PCMI测量对齐音素标签与SSL语音表示诱导出的聚类之间的互信息一致性；(2) WACS利用动态时间规整（DTW）比较重复单词的语音表示序列相似性，衡量单词实现的一致性。通过随机和系统性扰动验证指标退化特性，并在多语言数据集上交叉验证。

Result: PCMI和WACS在对齐扰动下表现出一致的退化趋势；在85种语言的FLEURS、45种语言的DoReCo手动标注对齐数据以及两种低资源语言上，两种指标都能有效区分高质量与低质量对齐，并与基于时间戳的对齐质量度量强相关。

Conclusion: SSL语音表示能够支持可扩展、无参考的强制对齐评估，所提出的指标以开源Python包形式发布，为大规模多语言强制对齐质量评估提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Forced alignment evaluation typically requires manually annotated timestamps, limiting large-scale and multilingual analysis. We introduce two corpus-level metrics based on self-supervised (SSL) speech representations for reference-free forced alignment evaluation: Phoneme-Cluster Mutual Information (PCMI) and Word Acoustic Consistency Score (WACS). PCMI measures agreement between aligned phoneme labels and clusters induced from SSL-speech representations, while WACS measures consistency of repeated word realizations using dynamic time warping similarity between word representation sequences. Using both random and systematic perturbations, we show that PCMI and WACS degrade consistently under alignment perturbations. We further analyze the metrics across multiple alignment systems on 85 languages from FLEURS, validate them against manually annotated alignments from 45 languages in DoReCo, and evaluate them on two phonologically complex low-resource languages. The metrics effectively separate high- and low-quality alignments and correlate strongly with timestamp-based alignment quality measures. Our results demonstrate that SSL-speech representations enable scalable, reference-free forced alignment evaluation. The metrics are available as an open-source Python package at https://github.com/mahesh-ak/forced-aligner-metrics.

</details>


### [133] [A Formal Limitation on Learning Human Language From Textual Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28560)
*Emily Cheng,Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度回答"听者能否仅凭话语形式恢复说话者意图"这一问题，推导出任意文本特征化器（包括大语言模型的隐状态）所能达到的解码成功概率上界。该上界由话语形式对意义的不确定性决定，分为不可约部分和仅能由外部语境消除的部分，对任何表示（无论由多少文本或监督产生）都成立，并在人工语言、汉语零代词消解和颜色指称任务中得到实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是回答一个基本的语言哲学与计算语言学问题：仅凭话语的形式（utterance form），听者能在多大程度上恢复说话者意图的意义？这个问题对所有基于文本表示的语义理解系统（包括大语言模型）都具有根本性意义，作者希望从信息论角度给出理论上限。

Method: 将语言使用建模为意义（meanings）、语境（contexts）和话语（utterances）的联合分布，对任意给定的文本特征化器（featurizer），推导解码器从话语表示中恢复说话者意图意义的概率上界。将不确定性分解为不可约部分和仅可由语境消除的部分，证明该界对离散或连续意义空间均成立。

Result: 得出了由话语形式对意义的固有不确定性决定的解码成功概率上界，该上界独立于具体表示或模型，任何表示都无法超越。在人工语言、中文零代词消解和颜色参照三个实验任务中提供了支持该理论的实证证据。

Conclusion: 结论是：仅凭话语形式无法完全恢复说话者的意义，存在一个语言内在的信息论下界。这一界对包括大语言模型隐状态在内的任何文本表示都适用，意味着即使拥有大量文本或监督数据，语义歧义中不可约部分仍无法仅靠形式解决，必须依赖外部语境。

Abstract: Can a listener recover what a speaker means from the form of an utterance alone? We answer this question information-theoretically, and for a listener given by any featurizer of text, including the hidden states of contemporary large language models. Modeling language use as a joint distribution over meanings, contexts, and utterances, we derive upper bounds on the probability that a decoder recovers a speaker's intended meaning from a representation of the utterance. The bounds are governed by the uncertainty that form leaves about meaning, which splits into an irreducible part and a part that only (extralinguistic) context, but never the utterance alone, can resolve. Because these quantities are intrinsic to language, no representation, however much text or supervision produced it, can surpass them; the bounds hold whether the space of meanings is discrete or continuous. Experiments on artificial languages, Mandarin zero-pronoun resolution, and color reference provide empirical evidence in support of the theory.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Time Capsule of Testable Human Knowledge: 41 Years of Jeopardy! in a Single Free Local Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27459)
*David Noever,Forrest McKee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对比了2011年IBM Watson的DeepQA系统与现代开源大语言模型在Jeopardy!问答任务上的表现。作者在完整开放Jeopardy!数据集（529,939条线索，覆盖1984-2025年共41季）上评估了单台9GB的开源权重模型（Qwen2.5-14B，4-bit量化），该模型在严格强制回答协议下达到67.0%的准确率，事实型类别超过85%。在训练截止日期之后播出的线索上，本地模型保持65%的准确率，Claude Opus 4.8达到95%，而Watson因构建时冻结的语料库得零分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在证明知识型AI系统从大型专用基础设施（如Watson的服务器集群和精心策划的十亿文档语料库）向便携、廉价、持续更新的开源模型发生了根本性转变。通过对比Watson（构建时冻结的封闭语料库）与现代模型（可回答训练截止后新出现的问题），揭示文化可查询知识快照的可移植性变革。

Method: 作者在完整开放Jeopardy!线索数据集上评估Qwen2.5-14B模型（4-bit量化，9GB单文件），采用严格的强制回答协议，结合精确匹配和模糊匹配进行评分。分别评估模型在全部41季数据和训练截止日期之后播出的线索上的表现，并与Watson（按构造得零分）和Claude Opus 4.8进行对比。

Result: Qwen2.5-14B对全部529,939条线索的回答准确率为67.0%，事实型类别（factoid categories）超过85%。在训练截止之后播出的线索上，本地模型保持65%的准确率，Claude Opus 4.8达到95%，而Watson因构建时无法获取未来数据而为零分。

Conclusion: 知识型AI的能力已从需要大型专用基础设施的冻结系统转变为可封装于单个文件、可穿越时间更新、几乎零成本的便携系统。与Watson不同，现代模型不受限于其构建时刻，能够在训练截止后持续回答问题。训练数据暴露不应被视为语言模型独有的缺陷，因为Watson恰恰是更极端的案例——其语料库专门为目标答案构建并在过去线索上调优，且无法回答策划分布之外的任何内容。

Abstract: In 2011, IBM's Watson was something like a sealed capsule of its era's queryable knowledge. Its DeepQA system defeated the strongest human Jeopardy! champions, but the knowledge that let it do so lived in a curated billion-document corpus running on a cluster of POWER7 servers, frozen at build time and impossible to move or copy. We show that the same kind of artifact, a snapshot of what a culture can answer, is now portable and essentially free. We evaluate a single 9 GB open-weight model (Qwen2.5-14B, 4-bit) against the complete open Jeopardy! clue dataset, 529,939 clues across all 41 broadcast seasons from 1984 to 2025. To our knowledge this is the first time a model has been run over the full corpus. The 41 years mark only how long the questions were collected. What they test is far older and broader: the accumulated body of human general knowledge a culture considers worth knowing, from ancient history and dead languages to science, literature, and geography, with a verified answer for every item. The model answers 67.0% of all clues under a strict forced-response protocol with exact and fuzzy matching, and exceeds 85% on factoid categories. We treat training-data exposure as something both systems share rather than a flaw unique to language models. Watson's case is in fact the more extreme one. Its corpus was assembled to contain Jeopardy answers and it was tuned on past clues, and it could not answer anything outside that curated distribution. The decisive test is whether a model can answer clues that did not exist when it was built. On clues aired after its training cutoff, the local model holds 65% and Claude Opus 4.8 holds 95%, while Watson by construction scores zero. The capability survives the move from a server room to a file you could seal in a time capsule, and unlike Watson it is not frozen to its own moment.

</details>


### [135] [Rating the Raters: Rasch Measurement Theory for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27463)
*Pratik S. Sachdeva,Nathan Boudol*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将Rasch测量理论（RMT）应用于LLM评估范式，以分解测量中各核心组件（被试、题目、评分者）对结果的贡献。通过对九个LLM在仇恨言论语料上的多面Rasch模型拟合，发现LLM与人类评分者在严格程度、题目校准、顺序稳健性、目标身份敏感性和量表使用上存在系统性差异，而这些差异在标准评估实践中会被掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM如今同时作为被评估者、法官和评分者参与评估，但这些范式本质上都是测量问题。标准评估实践往往忽视被试、题目和评分者三个核心组件各自对最终结果的贡献，导致我们无法准确理解究竟在测量什么，因此需要更合适的测量框架。

Method: 采用Rasch测量理论（RMT），将序数评分分解为共享尺度上的可分离层面。使用Measuring Hate Speech语料库（该语料库本身即在RMT框架下构建），对来自九个不同家族和能力水平的LLM的标注数据拟合一系列多面Rasch模型（many-facet Rasch models），并与人类评分者进行对比分析。

Result: LLMs与人类评分者在五个维度上存在系统性差异：严重性判断的严格程度、题目层面的校准、问题顺序的稳健性、目标身份敏感性以及评分量表的使用方式。这些差异在标准评估实践中都会被掩盖，表明仅看表面评分会遗漏关键的测量偏差。

Conclusion: RMT应当纳入LLM评估（包括LLM-as-examinee、LLM-as-judge和LLM-as-rater三种范式）的标准工具箱，因为它能提供标准评估实践无法捕获的诊断信息，帮助识别测量校准问题和评分者偏见。

Abstract: LLMs now sit on every side of evaluation: as examinees scored on benchmarks, judges of other models' outputs, and raters of human-generated content. Each paradigm can be viewed as a measurement problem, where a latent property of an object is probed with items from an instrument (e.g., benchmark) by raters. Standard evaluation practices often neglect the contributions of each core component to the end result, limiting our understanding of what is being measured. Rasch measurement theory (RMT) is well-suited to this kind of problem. RMT decomposes ordinal ratings into separable facets on a common scale. It further provides a battery of diagnostics that can identify miscalibrated measurements and rater biases. We present a case study of RMT applied to the LLM-as-rater paradigm using the Measuring Hate Speech corpus, whose construct was itself built under RMT. We fit a series of many-facet Rasch models to annotations from nine LLMs spanning families and capability levels. Our analyses show that LLMs systematically differ from human raters in severity, item-level calibration, question-order robustness, target-identity sensitivity, and rating scale use, which all would be obscured by standard evaluation practice. Overall, we argue that RMT belongs in the toolkit for evaluating LLM-as-examinee, -judge, and -rater paradigms.

</details>


### [136] [Not All Explanations Are Sought: Information-Seeking Psychology for Human-Centered XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27464)
*Andrea Beretta,Salvatore Rinzivillo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出人本可解释AI（HCXAI）应融合信息寻求心理学，基于Sharot和Sunstein的动机框架，从工具性、享乐性和认知性三种预期效用的角度来理解用户是否愿意与解释互动，同时考虑多种认知偏差导致的过度或不足信息寻求问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前HCXAI主要关注如何提高解释的可用性和质量，但忽略了用户是否有动力去主动寻求解释这一根本问题。尤其在代理型AI系统中，解释不仅要帮助理解单个输出，还要支持预测级联行动、评估风险和决定干预时机，这使得理解"用户何时及为何想要知道"变得尤为关键。

Method: 采用立场论文的方法，整合Sharot和Sunstein的信息寻求动机框架（工具性、享乐性、认知性三种预期效用），并引入六种认知偏差（控制幻觉、自动化偏差、不切实际的乐观、影响偏差、过度自信和确认偏差）来分析这些偏差如何扭曲效用的估计，从而产生两种失败模式：过度信息寻求和不足信息寻求。

Result: 识别出两个关键失败模式：(1) 过度信息寻求导致注意力碎片化且无法改善决策；(2) 不足信息寻求导致关键风险和误解未被审查。指出该问题在代理型AI系统中尤为严重，因为解释需要支持对级联行动的预测、风险评估和干预决策。

Conclusion: 主张HCXAI的范式应从"使解释可用"转向"使解释被寻求"，即设计系统时需考虑用户实际何时以及为什么会主动寻求信息，从而更有效地支持人机协作中的决策和风险管控。

Abstract: This position paper argues that human-centered explainable AI (HCXAI) should incorporate insights from the psychology of information seeking. Drawing on Sharot and Sunstein's framework of information-seeking motives, we propose that people evaluate whether to engage with explanations based on three types of expected utility: instrumental (will it help me act better?), hedonic (will it make me feel better?), and cognitive (will it improve my understanding?). Each utility is estimated through a lens shaped by well-documented cognitive biases, including illusion of control, automation bias, unrealistic optimism, impact bias, overconfidence, and confirmation bias. These biases can lead to two failure modes: excessive information-seeking that fragments attention without improving decisions, and insufficient information-seeking that leaves critical risks and misunderstandings unexamined. This challenge is particularly acute for agentic AI systems, where explanations must support not just understanding a single output but anticipating cascading actions, assessing risks, and deciding when to intervene. By integrating information-seeking psychology into HCXAI, we advocate for a shift from making explanations available to making them sought: designing systems that account for when and why users actually want to know.

</details>


### [137] [Retrieving Relations, Detecting Fallacies: A RAG Approach to Political Debate Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27471)
*Deborah Dore,Greta Damo,Elena Cabrio,Serena Villata*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于引导式检索增强的谬误检测与分类方法，利用论证结构中的支持与攻击关系动态引导相关文档的检索，在ElecDeb60to20基准上，通过42种检索配置和14个模型在15GB政治文档知识库上进行评估，谬误检测macro-F1达0.864，分类达0.725，显著优于无检索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谬误论证的自动检测在政治辩论等敏感场景中至关重要，但仅凭表面文本难以识别，需要世界知识和论证间的关系。先前的论证结构编码方式仅作为静态分类器特征，灵活性有限，无法动态利用结构信息来改善检测性能。

Method: 提出一种引导式检索增强（guided retrieval-augmented）方法论，利用论证结构中的支持与攻击关系来动态指引相关文档的检索过程，从而为谬误检测和分类提供外部知识支持。

Result: 在ElecDeb60to20基准上，使用15GB政治文件知识库，涵盖42种检索配置和14种模型。谬误检测macro-F1达到0.864，分类macro-F1达到0.725，均优于非检索基线。

Conclusion: 引入外部知识并在检索过程中以论证结构进行引导，能显著提升谬误检测与分类的效果，验证了论证知识引导的检索增强方法的有效性。

Abstract: Fallacies are arguments that employ invalid reasoning, making their automatic detection critical in sensitive contexts such as high-stakes political debates, where public opinion is shaped. Spotting a fallacious argument requires contextual knowledge beyond its pure surface text. This entails world knowledge pertaining to the subject matter under discussion, as well as knowledge of the relationships that exist between arguments within the argumentative discourse. Prior work on fallacy analysis has shown that argumentative discourse structure can beneficially improve classification performance. However, such structure is typically encoded only as static classifier features, limiting its flexibility. Building on this intuition while addressing this limitation, we introduce a guided retrieval-augmented methodology for fallacy detection and classification that leverages argumentative relations of support and attack to dynamically steer the extraction of relevant documents. We evaluate our approach on the ElecDeb60to20 benchmark across 42 retrieval configurations and 14 models, performing retrieval over a 15GB knowledge base of collected political-related documents. Our approach improves macro-F1 up to 0.864 for fallacy detection and up to 0.725 for classification over non-retrieval baselines. These results show that incorporating external knowledge significantly enhances fallacy detection and classification when retrieval is argumentatively guided.

</details>


### [138] [LLM-Augmented Causal Discovery: Probabilistic Fusion of Edge Existence and Orientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27472)
*Neville K. Kitson,Anthony Constantinou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的表示方法——概率依赖图（PDGs），通过将贝叶斯网络结构学习（BNSL）算法与大型语言模型（LLM）的因果知识进行加权融合，来提升因果图结构学习的准确性。在26个基准网络上，简单的50/50融合在22个网络上优于单一来源，平均F1提升0.056（p<0.001）。两种来源互补：BNSL提供高召回率的边骨架（80% vs LLM的60%），而LLM提供高精度的边方向（96% vs BNSL的77%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯网络结构学习（BNSL）从观测数据中学习时面临方向可识别性（orientation identifiability）的固有困难，即仅凭数据难以确定因果边的方向。而大型语言模型（LLM）虽具备广泛的因果知识，但输出往往不可靠或不一致。两种来源存在天然的互补性，但如何将它们有机融合仍然是一个未解决的挑战。

Method: 提出概率依赖图（PDG）表示：每条边关联一个基于有向、无向和不存在三种状态的概率分布，通过加权平均实现BNSL和LLM结果的融合。实验设置了26个基准网络，组合三种BNSL算法（FGES、Tabu、PC）和三种LLM（Gemini、Claude、GPT），使用多个提示词和随机种子进行评估。

Result: 简单的50/50融合在26个基准网络中有22个网络获得优于单一来源的F1分数，平均F1提升0.056，且具有统计学显著性（p<0.001）。分析表明两种来源互补：BNSL提供高召回率的边骨架（80% vs LLM的60%），LLM提供高准确率的边方向（96% vs BNSL的77%）。

Conclusion: 将两种互补来源表示为关于边存在与方向的不确定性的概率分布，并通过加权平均融合，是一种实用且有效的提升因果图准确性的方法。该工作验证了数据驱动方法与知识驱动方法结合的潜力。

Abstract: Bayesian network structure learning (BNSL) from observational data struggles with orientation identifiability, while large language models (LLMs) offer broad but often unreliable causal knowledge. We propose combining these complementary sources through a novel representation, termed Probabilistic Dependency Graphs (PDGs). In a PDG, each edge is associated with a distribution over directed, undirected, and absent states, enabling fusion via weighted averaging. We evaluate this approach on 26 benchmark networks, combining ensembles of three BNSL algorithms (FGES, Tabu, PC) with three LLMs (Gemini, Claude, GPT) across multiple prompts and random seeds. A simple 50/50 fusion improves F1 over the better of either source alone in 22 of 26 networks, with a statistically significant mean improvement of $0.056$ $(p<0.001)$. Analysis reveals that the two sources play complementary roles: BNSL contributes a high-recall edge skeleton (80\% vs 60\% for LLM), while LLM contributes accurate edge orientation (96\% vs 77\% for BNSL). Our results show that representing both sources as probabilistic uncertainty over edge existence and orientation is a practical and effective way to improve causal graph accuracy.

</details>


### [139] [Hypothesize, Evaluate, Refine: A Scientific Agent for PDE Discovery with Unknown Spatial Coefficient Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27475)
*YuJie Huang,WenWu He,ZhuoEr Lin,Congcong Liu,Dong Liang,Zhuo-Xu Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HER-PDE框架，一个科学智能体系统，用于在异质介质中发现偏微分方程——联合识别控制算子及其非参数化的空间变系数场。该系统通过分析两条不同激励下的含噪轨迹，结合结构化假设生成与局部细化，在五个二维控制系统中均恢复出生成算子，并在未知系数场上达到约0.85的中位Pearson相关性和约0.28的中位相对L2误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异质介质中发现PDE需要同时识别控制算子和参数化该算子的未知空间场，两者高度耦合：场布置改变微分定律，而足够灵活的场可在单一轨迹上掩盖结构误差。现有方法缺乏同时处理这两个耦合任务的有效手段，因此需要一个能联合发现组合PDE结构与非参数时不变系数场的智能框架。

Method: HER-PDE采用"假设-评估-细化"的科学研究循环：(1) 智能体分析由不同激励产生的两条含噪轨迹；(2) 提出完整的表达式树假设；(3) 结合创造性结构探索与局部候选细化；(4) 假设评估接口(HEI)仅估计每个假设中显式声明的场，绝不添加缺失项，并通过双向交叉激励迁移对结构评分；(5) 选定的定律在密封的时间区间上进行验证审计。

Result: 在五个受控的二维系统中（5%相对高斯状态噪声），智能体在所有五个案例中都恢复了生成算子，包括等价的符号场和乘积规则参数化。在九个未知系数场上，恢复的场达到约0.85的中位Pearson相关性和约0.28的中位相对L2误差。

Conclusion: 智能体引导的假设细化能够在不对空间系数预设参数形式的情况下恢复异质控制定律，证明该框架可有效处理PDE发现中控制算子与未知场联合识别这一耦合难题。

Abstract: Discovering PDEs in heterogeneous media requires jointly identifying the governing operator and the unknown spatial fields that parameterize it. These tasks are coupled: changing field placement changes the differential law, while a sufficiently flexible field can conceal structural error on a single trajectory. We present Hypothesize, Evaluate, Refine for PDE Discovery (HER-PDE), a scientific-agent framework that discovers compositional PDE structure together with nonparametric, time-invariant coefficient fields. The Agent analyzes two noisy trajectories generated by different excitations, proposes complete expression-tree hypotheses, and combines creative structural exploration with local candidate refinement. Its Hypothesis Evaluation Interface (HEI) estimates only the fields explicitly declared in each hypothesis, never adds missing terms, and scores structures by bidirectional cross-excitation transfer. The selected law is subsequently audited on a sealed temporal interval. Across five controlled two-dimensional systems observed with 5 percent relative Gaussian state noise, the Agent recovers the generating operator in all five cases, including equivalent signed-field and product-rule parameterizations. Across nine unknown coefficient fields, the recovered fields attain a median Pearson correlation of approximately 0.85 and a median relative L2 error of approximately 0.28. These results show that agent-guided hypothesis refinement can recover heterogeneous governing laws without prescribing a parametric form for their spatial coefficients.

</details>


### [140] [Class-Based Heuristic Selection for Solving the Flying Block Puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27476)
*Sanyar Ahmadi,Pedram Asadzadeh,Amanj Khorramian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CBHA*算法，通过将启发式搜索与基于状态空间类别的运动学分类相结合，在双列飞行拼图（Flying Block Puzzle）这一NP完全空间规划基准上实现了93.4%的求解成功率，相比标准A*提升显著，节点扩展减少87.98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用启发式搜索算法无法利用受限空间领域的结构约束，在较难的实例上性能急剧退化。而现实应用中（如多智能体路径规划、自动驾驶导航、块体搬移系统）普遍存在间隙-尺寸约束的空间规划问题，需要一种能够结构化利用这些约束的启发式机制。

Method: 提出类基启发式A*（CBHA*）：1) 引入通用移动约束（General Move Constraint）以捕获空闲单元稀缺时的最小位移成本；2) 建立运动学分类体系，将状态空间划分为七个互斥类别，并基于空置率和目标块几何形状构造可采纳启发式；3) 设计类别条件平局打破机制，在深度优先和垂直距离排序之间动态切换以克服f值平台。

Result: 在146个基准实例上，CBHA*求解成功率达93.4%，远优于深度优先A*（64%）、标准A*（39%）和BFS（17%）；相对标准A*节点扩展减少87.98%，平均有效分支因子约为3。

Conclusion: 类别触发的自适应启发式是高效空间规划的一种原则性机制，能够结构化地推广到各类物理约束系统，为受限空间中的智能体规划提供了新的方法论。

Abstract: Heuristic search underlies planning in autonomous systems ranging from warehouse logistics to robotic navigation, yet generic heuristics fail to exploit the structural constraints that govern constrained spatial domains, causing search performance to degrade catastrophically on harder instances. We study this problem through the two-column Flying Block Puzzle, a rigorously NP-complete spatial planning microworld whose bottleneck geometry mirrors clearance-to-size constraints encountered in multi-agent path finding, autonomous vehicle navigation, and block relocation systems. We introduce the Class-Based Heuristic A* (CBHA*) algorithm, which integrates a General Move Constraint to capture minimum displacement costs when vacant units are scarce, a formal kinematic taxonomy partitioning the state space into seven mutually exclusive classes with provably admissible heuristics based on vacancy ratio and goal-piece geometry, and a class-conditional tie-breaking mechanism that dynamically switches between depth-priority and vertical-distance ordering to overcome f-value plateaus. Over 146 benchmark instances, CBHA* achieves a 93.4% success rate against 64% for Depth-Prioritized A*, 39% for Standard A*, and 17% for BFS, while reducing node expansions by 87.98% relative to Standard A* and sustaining an average effective branching factor of approximately 3, demonstrating that class-triggered adaptive heuristics constitute a principled mechanism for efficient spatial planning that generalizes structurally to physical constraint systems.

</details>


### [141] [Benchmarking General Mobile Assistants in Challenging Real-World Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27477)
*Yiqi Zhu,Feiyu Gao,Jiaxing Fan,Jiahui Zeng,Minggang Wu,Chenliang Li,Haiyang Xu,Peng Li,Ming Yan,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GMA基准，一个用于评估通用移动助手在现实世界复杂场景中能力的benchmark。GMA基于开源项目引入7个应用，涵盖生活分享和旅行规划等领域，包含300个任务、4个难度层级。作者评估了8个前沿模型，发现性能随任务复杂度增加显著下降。通过受控消融实验，发现合适的harness设计（如上下文保留和显式状态跟踪）可以显著提升性能，尤其在复杂工作流上，但效果因基础模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准（如AndroidWorld和MobileWorld）虽为移动agent评估提供了坚实基础，但其应用覆盖范围和任务设计未能充分涵盖现实移动使用的多样性和复杂性。亟需一个更具挑战性和更全面的基准来推动移动agent在真实场景中的发展。

Method: GMA基准包含：(1) 7个基于开源项目的应用，覆盖生活分享、旅行规划等真实领域；(2) 300个任务，划分为4个难度层级（从原子操作到复杂的多步骤工作流）；(3) 评估8个前沿模型；(4) 在共享环境、模型设置和任务分类下进行受控消融实验，研究harness设计（如上下文保留和显式状态跟踪）的影响。

Result: 当前agent的性能随任务复杂度增加而显著下降，远不能可靠处理真实用户需求。受控消融实验表明，恰当的harness设计能有效提升性能，特别是在高要求的工作流中，但特定设计的有效性在不同基础模型间存在差异。

Conclusion: GMA通过扩展应用覆盖范围和任务复杂度，补充了现有基准的不足，为评估移动agent以及在复杂移动工作流中研究harness设计如何支持可靠执行提供了具有挑战性的测试平台。

Abstract: Graphical user interfaces have emerged as an important environment for evaluating autonomous AI agents on multimodal interactive tasks. Existing benchmarks such as AndroidWorld and MobileWorld provide strong foundations for mobile agent evaluation, but their application coverage and task design do not yet fully capture the diversity and complexity of realistic mobile use. We present GMA, a benchmark for evaluating general mobile assistants in challenging real-world scenarios. GMA introduces seven applications based on open-source projects, spanning domains such as lifestyle sharing and travel planning, and 300 tasks across four difficulty tiers, from atomic actions to complex multi-step workflows. We evaluate eight frontier models and find that performance declines substantially as task complexity increases, with current agents remaining far from reliably handling realistic user requirements. We further conduct controlled ablation studies of agent harness choices, including context retention and explicit state tracking, under a shared environment, model setting, and task taxonomy. Results show that appropriate harness design can meaningfully improve performance, particularly on demanding workflows, while the effectiveness of specific designs can vary across foundation models. Overall, GMA complements existing benchmarks by expanding application coverage and task complexity, providing a challenging testbed for evaluating mobile agents and studying how harness design supports reliable execution in complex mobile workflows.

</details>


### [142] [Effectiveness of IoT and Deep Learning for Detection and Severity Assessment of Postelectrotermes militaris in Tea Plantations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27480)
*D. K. C. Senevirathna,A. A. E. Nanayakkara,H. M. C. K. Kulathunga,J. K. D. P. Nadula,R. M. Mapatuna,Malithi Nawarathne,Jaliya L. Wijayaraja,P. D. Senanayake,Samitha Vidhanaarachchi,Kalpani Manathunga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物联网声学监测与深度学习的框架，用于茶园中上乡活木白蚁（ULWT）侵害的早期检测与严重程度评估。系统通过树莓派IoT设备采集茶树干的声学信号，利用傅里叶频谱图训练CNN进行分类，并结合声学幅度和邻近受害植株构建加权严重程度模型，实现了81.5%的分类准确率和地理空间可视化映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 茶园极易受到后翅颈白蚁（ULWT）侵害，当侵害未被及时发现时会造成重大损失。现有检测手段难以实现早期、非侵入性的白蚁侵害监测，因此需要一种能够在真实环境噪声下进行早期检测和严重程度评估的技术方案。

Method: 采用Raspberry Pi IoT设备连接高灵敏度麦克风，非侵入式采集茶树干的声学信号并记录地理坐标。信号经修剪、重采样和分段处理获得2,000个十秒样本（1,000健康、1,000受害），按1,600/200/200划分训练/验证/测试集。使用傅里叶频谱图训练CNN进行分类和侵害概率估计，构建加权严重性模型（结合CNN概率、平均声学幅度和5米内邻近受害植株），并通过地理空间映射可视化侵害分布。

Result: 在Pundaluoya的ULWT受害茶园实地试验中，测试集上CNN达到81.5%准确率、80.6%精确率、83.0%召回率、81.8% F1分数和0.819 ROC-AUC。系统在真实环境噪声下验证了可行性，并提供严重性定量评估和地理空间输出。

Conclusion: 该框架超越了简单的二元侵害检测，引入了基于侵害概率、声学幅度和邻近受害植株的定量严重性评估。严重性和地理空间输出可帮助茶园管理者识别高风险区域、优先安排田间巡查，并实施更及时和针对性的防治措施。

Abstract: Tea plantations are vulnerable to Postelectrotermes militaris, commonly known as the Upcountry Live Wood Termite (ULWT), which can cause substantial damage when infestations remain undetected. This study proposes an IoT-enabled acoustic monitoring framework integrated with deep learning for early detection and severity assessment of ULWT infestations in tea plantations.
  Research Method: Audio signals were captured non-invasively from tea trunks using a high-sensitivity microphone connected to a Raspberry Pi-based IoT device, with geographic coordinates recorded for spatial tracking. After trimming, resampling, and segmentation, 2,000 ten-second samples were obtained, comprising 1,000 healthy and 1,000 infested samples, and divided into 1,600 training, 200 validation, and 200 test samples. The dataset used in this study is publicly available on Kaggle (Senevirathna et al. 2026). Fourier-derived spectrograms trained a CNN for infestation classification and probability estimation. A weighted severity model combined CNN probability, mean acoustic amplitude, and nearby infested plants within 5 m, with geospatial mapping used to visualize infestation distribution.
  Findings and Values: Field trials in a ULWT-affected tea plantation in Pundaluoya demonstrated feasibility under realistic environmental noise. On the held-out test set, the CNN achieved 81.5% accuracy, 80.6% precision, 83.0% recall, 81.8% F1-score, and 0.819 ROC-AUC. Beyond binary infestation detection, the framework introduced quantitative severity assessment using infestation probability, acoustic amplitude, and nearby infested plants. The resulting severity and geospatial outputs can support plantation managers in identifying high-risk areas, prioritizing field inspections, and implementing more timely and targeted control measures.

</details>


### [143] [Context Localization for Generalized Level-Based Evaluation in Knowledge-Based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27482)
*Ondrej Hutník,Natália Puškárová*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究基于知识系统中广义层次评估的上下文局部化问题。作者提出了一种广义层次度量框架，该框架在满足规定聚合水平的可接受知识上下文上最大化单调集函数，并刻画了在什么条件下，通过上下文B过滤分数等价于通过与B的交集局部化可接受上下文。主要定理表明，对于所有单调集函数，这种一致性成立当且仅当满足两个结构条件：关于上下文的单调性和排除B外部正局部化支持的约简性质。作者进一步分析了产生约简性质的逐点和块生成机制，将结果扩展到参数化系统，并将其解释为上下文依赖证据选择、非加性支持评估和基于层次的知识聚合的稳定性准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在知识系统中，层次化评估（level-based evaluation）是评估事实、规则、案例、标准或证据单元的重要方法。现有框架缺乏对"上下文局部化"（context localization）的系统性理论刻画——即当分数被特定上下文B过滤时，如何保证可接受上下文集合也能相应地局部化。这涉及到知识聚合、非加性支持和证据选择的稳定性和一致性问题，需要建立严谨的形式化理论。

Method: 本文采用抽象数学形式化方法：首先建立广义层次度量的框架，定义在可接受知识上下文上的条件聚合测试；其次引入广义层次度量作为在所有达到规定聚合水平的上下文上最大化单调集函数的操作；然后给出一个刻画定理（主定理），证明过滤分数与局部化上下文等价的充要条件是两个结构条件（关于上下文的单调性和排除B外部正局部化支持的约简性质）；最后分析约简性质的逐点和块生成机制，并扩展到参数化系统。

Result: 作者证明了主要刻画定理：对于所有单调集函数，上下文B上的分数过滤与通过交集局部化可接受上下文之间的一致性成立，当且仅当满足上下文单调性和约简性质两个结构条件。约简性质排除了B外部的正局部化支持。此外，作者识别了产生约简性质的逐点机制（pointwise）和块生成机制（block-generated），并将结果推广到参数化系统族。

Conclusion: 本文为知识系统中的广义层次评估提供了上下文局部化的完整刻画，确立了稳定性的充分必要条件。这些结果可用于上下文依赖的证据选择、非加性支持评估和基于层次的知识聚合系统，为其一致性验证提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study context localization for generalized level-based evaluation in knowledge-based systems. The framework models situations where a structured nonnegative score, defined on facts, rules, cases, criteria or evidence units, is evaluated through conditional aggregation tests on admissible knowledge contexts. The generalized level measure maximizes a monotone set function over all contexts whose aggregated support reaches a prescribed level. We characterize when filtering the score by a context $B$ is equivalent to localizing the admissible contexts by intersection with $B$. The main theorem shows that this consistency holds for all monotone set functions if and only if two structural conditions are satisfied: monotonicity with respect to contexts and a reduction property excluding positive localized support outside $B$. We analyze pointwise and block-generated mechanisms producing the reduction property, extend the result to parameterized systems, and interpret it as a stability criterion for context-dependent evidence selection, non-additive support evaluation and level-based knowledge aggregation.

</details>


### [144] [CareGraph: An Auditable Hybrid AI Framework for Evidence-Grounded Personalized Longitudinal Health Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27484)
*Pratik Ghawate,Tanvi Patil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CareGraph，一个可审计的混合AI框架，用于将碎片化的临床、自报和可穿戴健康数据转化为有优先级的趋势、缺失上下文指标、有界下一步、讨论问题和带溯源链接的解释。该框架仅组织证据而不进行诊断、预测或临床决策。在合成队列上，趋势规则达到0.827准确率，缺失上下文检测达到0.815严格微F1（对比传统检测器的0.318），安全规则集达到0.974 F1。相较GPT 5.6基线，CareGraph更快、输出更短且与纵向目标表现出更好的探索性词法对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化医疗正被AI变革，但临床记录、患者自报和可穿戴设备产生的异构证据难以解释和溯源，碎片化信息缺乏结构化整合与可审计性。

Method: CareGraph采用混合AI流水线，包括确定性分析、上下文检测、图构建、约束语言模型综合、证据验证、安全控制和发布门控。通过严格约束不越界到诊断、预测或治疗决策，并以图结构管理证据溯源和确定性检索。

Result: 在400人合成队列上，冻结的普通最小二乘趋势规则配充分性门控达到0.827准确率、0.837宏F1（95%置信区间0.819-0.854）和0.974信息不足F1。缺失上下文检测0.815严格微F1对比传统检测器0.318。安全规则集v1.2在作者标注基准上达到1.000精度、0.950召回和0.974 F1。审计测试80名患者中79个综合和78个展示无回退，1个被阻止、1个因无效证据键闭锁。对比GPT 5.6在56名匹配患者上，CareGraph更快（40.15 vs 49.62秒）、更短（661 vs 1163词）且词法对齐更好。

Conclusion: CareGraph提供了一个以安全为边界的个性化健康系统基础，通过图审计验证了溯源和确定性检索；但增量图对生成的影响仍需配对评估。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is transforming personalized healthcare, yet fragmented clinical, self reported, and wearable evidence remains difficult to interpret and trace. We present CareGraph, an auditable hybrid AI framework that converts heterogeneous records into prioritized trends, missing context indicators, bounded next steps, discussion questions, and provenance linked explanations. CareGraph organizes evidence without diagnosing, predicting outcomes, selecting treatment, or making autonomous clinical decisions. Its pipeline covers deterministic analysis, context detection, graph construction, constrained language model synthesis, evidence validation, safety controls, and release gating. Tests used synthetic cohorts of 400 patients each for development, validation, and holdout. On holdout data, a frozen ordinary least squares trend rule with a sufficiency gate achieved 0.827 accuracy, 0.837 macro F1 with a 95 percent confidence interval of 0.819 to 0.854, and 0.974 insufficient data F1. Missing context detection achieved 0.815 strict micro F1 versus 0.318 for the legacy detector. On an authored holdout benchmark, safety ruleset version 1.2 achieved 1.000 precision, 0.950 recall, and 0.974 F1. An audit requiring graph retrieval across 80 patients yielded 79 syntheses and 78 presentations without fallback; one output was blocked and one failed closed because of an invalid evidence key. Against monolithic GPT 5.6 on 56 matched patients, CareGraph was faster at 40.15 versus 49.62 seconds, shorter at 661 versus 1,163 words, and showed better exploratory lexical alignment with longitudinal targets; the baseline used fewer tokens and cited more raw evidence. Graph auditing verified provenance and deterministic retrieval; incremental graph effects on generation require paired evaluation. CareGraph offers a safety bounded foundation for intelligent personalized health systems.

</details>


### [145] [Thinking Costs Tokens: When More Structure is Worth the Price](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27506)
*Thomas Nolasque,John Grey,Calista Pham,Ankit Vani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型中推理结构（搜索、验证、修订）的预算开销是否受token预算阈值影响。在FinQA和TAT-QA金融推理任务上，使用GPT-5.4 mini对单体模型与验证搜索架构进行对比（14档预算、28,000个案例），发现交叉点在1,000至1,500个输出等效token之间：低于该阈值时推理开销损害性能，高于该阈值时验证搜索架构持续优于单体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理结构（搜索、验证、修订）会消耗本就有限的token预算，但现有研究未系统考察预算阈值对这类开销收益的影响，即存在一个临界点——低于它规划与验证的开销会损害性能，高于它则能带来收益。

Method: 在两个金融推理数据集（FinQA和TAT-QA）上，使用GPT-5.4 mini在14档预算（250至42,000个输出等效token）下评估两个系统：单体模型（单次LLM调用）与验证搜索架构（增加规划、标签盲检和修复能力）。每档运行1,000个案例，共28,000个完成单元，并使用严格的交集-并集检验确认交叉点。

Result: 两个系统在最低两档预算下均得分为0%（无法容纳完整提示）。在1,000 token时单体模型达到18%准确率而验证搜索接近0%（规划开销挤占答案空间）。从1,500 token起，验证搜索超越单体模型并保持持续优势，最高档约44% vs 约40%。交叉点位于1,000至1,500个输出等效token之间，经严格交集-并集检验确认（两端点p≤0.001）。

Conclusion: 存在明确的token预算阈值（约1,000-1,500输出等效token），低于该阈值时推理开销的规划与验证成本超过其收益，高于该阈值时验证搜索架构带来持续优势。这为实际部署中选择推理架构（单体还是验证搜索）提供了明确的预算指导依据。

Abstract: Adding inference structure to a language model lets it search, verify, and revise, but these actions consume the very budget they are supposed to use well. In this paper, we investigate whether there exists a token-budget threshold, below which the overhead of planning and verification hurts performance and above which it helps. We evaluate two systems on FinQA and TAT-QA financial reasoning tasks, using GPT-5.4 mini across 14 budget tiers ranging from 250 to 42,000 output-equivalent tokens. The first system is a monolith, which is a single LLM call. The second is a verified search architecture that adds planning, label-blind checking, and repair capabilities. We run 1,000 cases for a total of 28,000 completed cells. Both systems score 0% at the two lowest tiers, where neither can fit a complete prompt. At 1,000 tokens, the monolith reaches 18% accuracy while verified search scores near 0%, since the planning overhead leaves no room for an answer. From 1,500 tokens onward, verified search surpasses the monolith and maintains a consistent advantage, reaching approximately 44% at the highest tiers while the monolith reaches approximately 40%. The crossover occurs between 1,000 and 1,500 output-equivalent tokens, confirmed by a strict intersection-union test ($p \le 0.001$ at both endpoints).

</details>


### [146] [WM-R1: Training GUI Agents to Reason and leverage World Models with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27508)
*Yu Han,Tianwen Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WM-R1，这是首个使用世界模型而非真实环境来训练移动GUI智能体的强化学习框架。WM-R1在训练过程中用世界模型替代真实Android环境作为状态转移来源，并将世界模型嵌入到智能体的思考过程中，使其能在执行动作前推演候选动作的后果。该方法消除了真实环境交互的需求，支持大规模并行化和步级粒度化的轨迹生成，并引入多维规则奖励来联合优化任务成功率、轨迹效率和世界模型利用率。实验表明WM-R1显著优于GRPO-only基线和推理时模拟方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI智能体的强化学习（RL）训练通常需要大量真实环境交互，导致高昂的资源成本和训练不稳定，尤其在GUI场景中问题更为突出。现有方法依赖真实Android环境进行rollout，不仅效率低、成本高，而且难以大规模并行化。因此需要一种无需真实环境交互的RL训练框架，以降低训练成本并提升训练稳定性。

Method: WM-R1通过以下核心设计实现世界模型驱动的GUI智能体训练：(1) 在世界模型而非真实环境中进行所有rollout，世界模型充当状态转移的来源；(2) 将世界模型嵌入智能体的推理/思考过程，使智能体能在执行最终动作前推演候选动作的后果；(3) 支持大规模并行化和步级粒度化的轨迹生成；(4) 引入多维规则奖励，联合优化任务成功率、轨迹效率与世界模型利用率；(5) 精心构建包含2000个具有挑战性任务的高质量数据集用于高效训练。

Result: 在Android移动平台上进行的基准测试实验表明，WM-R1训练的智能体在性能上显著超越了GRPO-only基线和推理时模拟方法，验证了世界模型驱动的无真实环境RL训练框架的有效性。

Conclusion: WM-R1证明了世界模型可以完全替代真实环境进行GUI智能体的强化学习训练，大幅降低了资源成本和训练不稳定性，同时通过将世界模型嵌入推理过程进一步提升了智能体的决策质量。这一框架为移动GUI智能体的高效训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: GUI agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) have showcased strong environment learning capabilities on mobile platforms. However, RL typically demands extensive real-environment interactions, leading to high resource costs and instability, especially in GUI scenarios. To address these, we propose WM-R1, the first reinforcement learning framework that trains mobile GUI agents with world models instead of real environments. Specifically, world models serve as the source of state transitions during all rollouts, replacing the real Android environment within the training loop. WM-R1 also embeds world models directly into the thinking process, enabling agents to reason about the consequences of candidate actions before committing to the final action. Crucially, WM-R1 eliminates the need for real-environment interaction, supports massively parallelized and step-level granularized trajectory generation grounded in world models, and introduces a multi-dimensional rule-based reward that jointly optimizes task success, trajectory efficiency, and world model utilization. For efficient training, we curate a high-quality dataset of 2000 challenging tasks. Experiments on Android mobile benchmarks demonstrate that WM-R1-trained agents significantly outperform GRPO-only baselines and inference-time simulation methods. Code is available at https://github.com/genalyu/WM-R1 .

</details>


### [147] [SETU: An Agentic Ecosystem for Multilingual, Persona-Aware Communication Coaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27524)
*Jonnalagadda Maruthi Tejas,Uponika Barman Roy,Tilottama Goswami,Samir Goswami,Mousita Dhar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SETU是一个面向企业沟通辅导的智能体生态系统，将视频、音频、文本相关性、评分、通知和报告等专业智能体通过可信编排协调，生成模态归因的辅导报告，用于多语言企业沟通训练。论文聚焦于销售场景并报告了该场景下的评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业培训团队需要可扩展、可解释的工具来改善多语言环境下的工作沟通。现有系统要么孤立地评估文本、音频或视频，要么产生难以审计的黑箱输出，不适合教练辅导使用。

Method: SETU将分析分解为专门的智能体（视频、音频-语音、文本相关性、评分、通知和报告），通过可信感知编排进行协调，生成具有模态归因的辅导报告，人类审阅者保留最终判断权。

Result: 论文报告了场景(ii)（面向目标受众适应的销售演讲）的评估结果，但未涉及场景(i)（招聘者-候选人资格与兴趣通话）的评估，原因是评估资源有限。

Conclusion: SETU通过模块化智能体架构和模态归因报告，为多语言企业沟通辅导提供了可审计、可扩展的解决方案，其名称（多种印度语言中的"桥"）体现了弥合区域语言与受众期望间沟通鸿沟的目标。

Abstract: Corporate training teams need scalable and explainable tools to improve workforce communication in multilingual settings. Existing systems often score text, audio, or video in isolation, or produce black-box outputs that are difficult to audit for coaching use. This paper presents SETU, an agentic ecosystem for corporate communication coaching aimed at recruiters, frontline sales professionals and training units who prepare for audience specific conversations. SETU is designed for two scoped scenarios: (i) recruiter-candidate eligibility-and-interest calls with persona context and (ii) sales pitches with target-audience adaptation; owing to limited evaluation resources, this paper reports results on scenario (ii) only. The ecosystem decomposes analysis into specialized video, audio-speech, text-relevance, scoring, notification and reporting agents coordinated through trust-aware orchestration. It generates modality-attributed coaching reports for formative training, with human reviewers retaining final judgment. The name SETU (bridge in several Indic languages) reflects the goal of bridging communication gaps across regional languages and audience expectations.

</details>


### [148] [Nemotron 3.5 Content Safety Moderator: A Compact Multimodal, Multilingual, and Reasoning Enabled Content Safety Moderator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27548)
*Varun Singh,Anuj Doshi,Makesh Narsimhan Sreedhar,Shaona Ghosh,Katherine Luna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NVIDIA 提出了 Nemotron 3.5 Content Safety Moderator (CS)，一个紧凑的 4B 视觉语言安全审核模型，能够同时审核用户提示、图像和助手回复，覆盖 12 种语言，支持低延迟的安全标签输出和可选的细粒度推理追踪，并配套发布了多模态多语言安全数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 应用的安全审核正在从纯文本扩展到多模态（图像、文档、截图），但现有护栏系统通常只覆盖部分场景，难以同时兼顾广覆盖、自定义策略控制和低成本，需要一种紧凑的统一安全审核方案。

Method: 构建了一个 4B 参数的视觉语言安全审核模型，可同时对用户提示、图像和助手响应进行分类审核，支持 12 种语言；提供低延迟的安全标签输出模式，以及在需要时输出简洁推理轨迹以应用自定义政策并识别违规类别。同时发布了包含人工标注真实图像、良性 VLM 与文档任务、合成稀有风险和越狱案例、自定义政策示例的多模态多语言训练数据集。

Result: 在涵盖多模态安全、文本审核、多语言鲁棒性、自定义策略遵循、良性误报和延迟的全面评估中，Nemotron 3.5 CS 在增加图像条件和策略条件审核能力的同时，与专门的护栏模型在广泛任务上保持竞争力，展现了实用的覆盖权衡。

Conclusion: 紧凑的视觉语言审核模型可以作为可部署的前线安全组件，其推理模式可选择性用于审计和策略审查，为生产环境中多模态 AI 安全审核提供了实际可行的方案。

Abstract: Safety moderation for deployed AI applications is moving beyond text-only prompts: systems increasingly need to judge images, documents, screenshots, and generated responses under policies that vary across domains. Existing guardrails usually cover only part of this setting, making it difficult to combine broad coverage, custom policy control, and low compute cost. We present Nemotron 3.5 Content Safety Moderator, also referred to as Nemotron 3.5 CS in this paper for brevity, a compact 4B vision-language safety moderator that jointly classifies user prompts, images, and assistant responses across 12 languages. Nemotron 3.5 CS returns safety labels for latency-sensitive moderation and can additionally produce concise reasoning traces that apply supplied custom policies and identify violated categories when reasoning is requested. We also release a multimodal and multilingual safety dataset for guard training, spanning human-labeled real-image moderation, benign vision-language and document tasks, synthetic rare-risk and jailbreak cases, and custom-policy examples. Across evaluations spanning multimodal safety, text moderation, multilingual robustness, custom-policy following, benign false positives, and latency, Nemotron 3.5 CS demonstrates a practical coverage tradeoff: it adds image-conditioned and policy-conditioned moderation while remaining broadly competitive with specialized guard models. These results suggest that compact vision-language moderators can serve as deployable front-line safety components, with reasoning used selectively for audit and policy review.

</details>


### [149] [LongGuard: Mechanistic Analysis and Training-Free Mitigation of Long-Context Failure in Safety Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27580)
*Ziyang Chen,Xing Wu,Songlin Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LongGuard是一个检测、机制分析并缓解大语言模型长上下文安全防护失效的框架。作者通过Safety Needle-in-a-Haystack (SafetyNIAH)任务在0.25k-32k长度网格上评估了15种主流防护装置，发现不安全内容召回率平均下降超过50%，并将失效归因于不安全"针"的比例稀释而非绝对长度。通过三层注意力-对数几率-行为分析定位了失效机制，提出两种无需训练的缓解方法（分块检测CD和注意力头锐化AHS）及部署协议CAHR，在五个基准上将六种防护装置的平均性能提升22%和13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全防护装置（guardrails）几乎完全在短文本上训练和评估，但在实际应用中LLM可能处理长上下文。长上下文中防护装置如何失效、为何失效、如何缓解，这些问题此前缺乏系统研究。

Method: (1) 将任务形式化为SafetyNIAH，在0.25k-32k长度网格上评估15种防护装置；(2) 设计配对的Benign-Fill与Needle-Repeat实验以区分比例稀释与绝对长度效应；(3) 对六种防护装置进行注意力-对数几率-行为三层机制分析，并隔离出防护特化的检索头；(4) 提出两种免训练缓解方法CD（分块检测）和AHS（注意力头锐化），以及部署协议CAHR（根据上下文长度和审计侧选择配置）。

Result: 15种主流防护装置在不安全内容召回率上平均下降超过50%，且呈单调下降趋势；失效归因于不安全针的比例稀释而非绝对长度。注意力-对数几率-行为链路一致：注意力质量被稀释→不安全/安全对数几率边距压缩→检测决策崩溃，且在控制长度后该链路仍然一致。CAHR-CD和CAHR-AHS在五个基准上将六种防护装置平均性能分别提升22%和13%。

Conclusion: 长上下文导致安全防护失效的根本机制是注意力稀释造成的检测信号衰减，而非长度本身；通过分块检测和注意力头锐化等免训练手段可有效恢复长上下文下的防护能力，为部署长上下文安全防护提供了可行的实用方案。

Abstract: Safety guardrails serve as the last line of defense against harmful inputs and outputs of large language models (LLMs), yet they are trained and evaluated almost exclusively on short text. We present LongGuard, a framework that evaluates, mechanistically analyzes, and mitigates long-context guardrail failure. We formulate the task as Safety Needle-in-a-Haystack (SafetyNIAH) over a 0.25k-32k length grid; across 15 mainstream guardrails, unsafe recall drops monotonically by more than 50% on average, and a paired Benign-Fill vs. Needle-Repeat design attributes the failure to proportional dilution of the unsafe needle rather than to absolute length. A three-layer attention-logit-behavior analysis on six guardrails locates the mechanism: attention mass on the unsafe needle is diluted, the unsafe-over-safe logit margin is compressed in lockstep, and the detection decision collapses accordingly, with this attention->logit->behavior chain remaining consistent after partialling out length. We further isolate a sparse set of guard-specialized retrieval heads that exhibit partial specificity relative to their base models. Building on the analysis, we propose two training-free mitigations - Chunked Detection (CD) and Attention-Head Sharpening (AHS) - and a deployment protocol, Context-Aware Hyperparameter Routing (CAHR), that selects configurations by context length and audit side. Across five benchmarks spanning synthetic data, long-context attacks, and reasoning-model outputs, CAHR-CD and CAHR-AHS improve the six-guardrail average by 22% and 13%, respectively. Code and data are available online.

</details>


### [150] [Generative AI Expands the Intellectual Reach of Course Based Undergraduate Research Experiences (CUREs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27638)
*Aditi Babar,Kristin J. Davin,Alex Dornburg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过三个学期的纵向定性数据，探讨了在基于课程的研究体验（CURE）中嵌入生成式人工智能（GenAI）的影响。研究发现GenAI通过三种方式扩展了研究体验的智力范围：个性化按需支架使学生将自身兴趣转化为可研究问题；GenAI成为研究团队分布式认知系统的一部分，促进跨专业领域的沟通协调；同时GenAI并未取代学科判断，学生通过验证、修订或拒绝AI生成内容来保留智力责任，从而维持真实科学研究中人类判断的核心地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CUREs通过教师支持使本科生能参与真实科学研究，但随着研究问题日益复杂，教师难以提供充分且适应性的个性化支持。生成式人工智能可能通过提供可适应学生需求变化的个性化辅助来弥补这一不足，但目前关于GenAI嵌入CURE对学生探究、协作和科学推理的影响尚不清楚，因此需要实证研究来解答这一关键问题。

Method: 研究采用纵向定性研究设计，在生物信息学和基因组学CURE课程中收集了跨越三个学期的定性数据。通过分析学生在研究过程中的探究行为、协作模式和科学推理表现，归纳出GenAI嵌入CURE后对研究体验产生的影响模式。

Result: 研究发现GenAI以三种方式扩展了CURE的智力范围：（1）提供个性化、按需的支架，使学生能超越教师专业知识的边界，将自身兴趣转化为可研究的问题，所有团队都开发了独立的自主选题项目而非选择教师提供的主题；（2）GenAI成为研究团队分布式认知系统的一部分，帮助新手研究者跨专业差异进行沟通与协调，同时不消除专业化分工；（3）扩展能力并未替代学科判断的需要——学生越来越主动地验证、修订或拒绝AI生成的贡献，研究独立性通过保留智力责任而出现。

Conclusion: GenAI能够扩展CURE的覆盖范围，通过扩大新手研究者可探究的问题范围、协作方式以及可承担的责任水平来增强研究体验，同时保留人类判断作为真实科学探究的核心要素。这表明GenAI有望成为CURE教学的有效辅助工具，且其最佳使用方式不是替代学生的思考，而是支持他们在保持智力责任的前提下拓展研究边界。

Abstract: Course-based undergraduate research experiences (CUREs) broaden access to authentic scientific inquiry through responsive instructor support as research problems become increasingly complex. Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) may extend this support by providing individualized assistance that can adapt as student needs change. However, how embedding GenAI within a CURE to provide support across the research process impacts student inquiry, collaboration, and scientific reasoning remains unresolved. Here we use longitudinal qualitative data collected across three semesters of a bioinformatics and genomics CURE to show that GenAI expanded the intellectual reach of the research experience in three distinct ways. First, personalized, on-demand scaffolding allowed students to move beyond the boundaries of instructor expertise and transform their own interests into researchable inquiry, with all teams developing distinct self-directed projects rather than selecting instructor-provided topics. Second, GenAI became part of the distributed cognitive system of research teams, helping novice researchers communicate and coordinate across differentiated expertise without eliminating specialization. Third, expanded capability did not replace the need for disciplinary judgment. Students increasingly validated, revised, or rejected AI-generated contributions, such that research independence emerged through retained intellectual responsibility. Together, these findings suggest that GenAI can extend the reach of CUREs by expanding what novice researchers can investigate, how they can collaborate, and the level of responsibility they can assume while preserving human judgment central to authentic scientific inquiry.

</details>


### [151] [If Agents Were Angels, No Governance Would Be Necessary: Out-of-Band Policy Enforcement at a Trusted Tool Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27646)
*Marc Millstone,Tyler Akidau,Johannes Brüderl,Marat Pekker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本篇论文提出了一种名为"带外策略执行"（OBPE）的机制，作为代理（agent）推理之外的可信安全边界，以解决LLM代理在获得用户凭证时可能通过越权访问、数据泄露等方式造成安全问题。OBPE在授权类型化操作和资源、在后端调用前窄化查询、以及在响应中过滤记录和字段或掩码值，并证明策略计划具有顺序无关性且代理策略无法扩大权限上限。在3,621次试验中，追踪失败率从57.6%降至0.2%，安全-有用完成率提升21.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代理获得用户凭证后，它会继承该用户的全部访问权限，却缺乏判断力来限制其使用。代理可以扫描每个可访问记录到模型上下文，隐藏指令可操纵其下一步调用，每次请求都保持凭证有效，而代理却可能超出其工作范围或吸收机密信息。基于提示（prompt）的防护是脆弱的护栏，因为只有一个易出错（fallible）的推理器来理解任务并执行其限制。

Method: OBPE在代理推理之外建立一个可信边界，具体包括：授权类型化操作和资源、在后端调用前窄化查询、过滤响应中的记录和字段、掩码值，以及基于参数值或外部状态的语义门控（可拒绝或挂起授权调用）。数据策略所有者设定最大授权范围，代理策略只能收窄而不能扩大。论文用Cedar策略语言构建HTTP代理原型，通过与Jira和ServiceNow模拟器对比，在四种模型上进行20项自适应红队任务的基准测试。

Result: 在3,621次试验中，追踪失败率（受保护数据进入代理上下文、答案中出现精确值或完成禁止效果）从57.6%降至0.2%，聚类加权降低41.2个百分点（95% CI: 27.7, 54.9）。任务完成率从79.1%降至60.9%，而配对的"安全-有用"完成率提升了21.8个百分点（95% CI: 9.5, 35.2）。同时发现了一些局限性：某些答案能在数据从未进入上下文的情况下重建值，或利用过滤后的行数作为神谕——即塑造单次执行并非无反干扰（noninterference）。写入控制、持久审批、时间性和聚合策略不在本评估范围内。

Conclusion: OBPE证明了将策略执行从代理推理中分离出来，可以大幅降低安全风险（追踪失败率从57.6%降至0.2%）并提高安全-有用完成率，说明可信边界比提示词护栏更有效。但研究也揭示了单次执行塑造与反干扰性之间的差距，以及现有评估范围之外仍存在写入控制、持久审批、时间性和聚合策略等需要进一步研究的方向。

Abstract: Give an agent a human's credential and it inherits the person's reach without the judgment that limits its use. It can sweep every reachable record into model context, where hidden instructions steer its next call, and every request stays credential-valid while the agent exceeds its job or absorbs a secret. Prompts are a brittle guardrail: one fallible reasoner interprets the task and enforces its limits.
  We present Out-of-Band Policy Enforcement (OBPE), a trusted boundary outside agent reasoning. It authorizes the typed operation and resource, narrows the query before the backend call, then filters records and fields or masks values in the response. Semantic gating can deny or hold an authorized call on argument values or external state. A data policy owner sets the maximum grant; agent policy can only narrow it. We prove, under stated conditions, that the policy plan is order-independent and agent policy cannot widen the ceiling. Field removal covers one execution; masking and history rules claim less.
  We release an HTTP proxy prototype simplified from our production system, with conformance tests tying its typed Cedar policy core to the model. Against Jira and ServiceNow mocks, our benchmark compares prompted agents with and without OBPE on four models, including 20 adaptive red-team tasks. A trace failure means protected data entered agent context, an exact value appeared in the answer, or a forbidden effect completed. In 3,621 trials it fell from 57.6% to 0.2%, a cluster-weighted reduction of 41.2 points [95% CI: 27.7, 54.9]; fulfillment fell from 79.1% to 60.9%, while paired safe-useful completion rose 21.8 points [9.5, 35.2]. Some answers reconstructed a value that never entered context or used filtered row counts as an oracle: shaping one execution is not noninterference. Write controls, durable approval, and temporal and aggregate policies lie outside this evaluation.

</details>


### [152] [A Framework for Object-Centric Predictive Monitoring of Collaborative Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27671)
*Daniel Calegari,Andrea Delgado,Leonel Peña,Martín Rubio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究将协作式跨组织流程的预测性流程监控（PPM）与对象中心流程挖掘（OCPM）相结合，提出了从扩展协作事件日志到OCEL 2.0对象中心表示的正式语义映射，并将协作预测任务重新表述为对象中心预测任务。在四个公开日志及BPI 2013衍生日志上，通过五种预测策略和十四项重构任务进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作式跨组织流程的预测性流程监控需要推理多个相互依赖的实体（参与者、消息、局部执行和全局协作案例），但现有方法仅将协作属性附加到传统事件日志中，仍保留单一案例视角，导致大量结构信息隐性化。OCPM通过将实体作为一等对象并显式建模关系和多案例概念，为此提供了替代方案。该研究旨在连接这两个领域。

Method: 论文提出三项贡献：(i) 建立从扩展协作事件日志到OCED兼容的对象中心表示的正式语义映射，并以OCEL 2.0格式序列化；(ii) 将协作预测任务重新表述为对象中心预测任务；(iii) 实现可复现的转换器和预测流水线。评估使用四种公开协作事件日志以及由BPI Challenge 2013事故管理日志衍生的第五个日志，执行十四个重构任务，采用五种预测策略，覆盖表格、序列和图原生编码。

Result: 该框架在五个日志上通过十四项任务和五种预测策略进行了系统评估。结果显示，对象中心表示使协作结构显式化，并使得基于对象关系的预测目标表达更为自然，超越了以案例为中心的既有分类体系。代价是增加了关系复杂性和对对象中心工具链的依赖。

Conclusion: 对象中心表示能够将协作流程的隐性结构显式化，并自然支持基于对象关系的预测目标设置，但带来了更高的关系复杂度和对对象中心专用工具的依赖性。论文同时讨论了该方法的优势、局限性及有效性威胁。

Abstract: Predictive Process Monitoring (PPM) of collaborative, inter-organizational processes requires reasoning over multiple interdependent entities, including participants, messages, local executions, and the global collaboration case. Existing approaches extend traditional event logs with collaboration attributes but retain a single-case perspective, leaving much of this structure implicit. Object-centric process mining (OCPM) provides an alternative by representing these entities as first-class objects with explicit relations and multiple notions of case. This study connects collaborative PPM and OCPM through three contributions: (i) a formal semantic mapping from extended collaborative event logs to an OCED-conformant object-centric representation, serialized in OCEL 2.0; (ii) a reformulation of collaborative prediction tasks as object-centric prediction tasks; and (iii) a reproducible converter and prediction pipeline implementing the proposed mapping. We evaluate the framework on four public collaborative event logs and a fifth derived from the BPI Challenge 2013 incident-management log by executing the fourteen reformulated tasks using five predictive strategies across tabular, sequential, and graph-native encodings. We further discuss the benefits, limitations, and threats to the approach's validity. The representation makes collaboration structure explicit and makes it natural to state prediction targets based on object relations that fall outside the case-centric taxonomy, at the cost of increased relational complexity and dependence on object-centric tooling.

</details>


### [153] [Agents for Everyone: A Workshop Framework for Building Agentic AI Capabilities in a Distributed Curation Community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27675)
*Seth Carbon,Sierra Moxon,Kimberly Van Auken,Pascale Gaudet,Christopher J. Mungall*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文描述了Gene Ontology Consortium为促进智能体AI在生物数据库策展中的应用，部署了基于JupyterHub和Claude Code的云端智能体环境，并开发了四个模块的互动培训工作坊。37名参与者参加了四小时的培训，核心发现是：在分布式科学社区中建立智能体AI能力，关键在于解决访问、工作流设计和培训问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI有潜力加速生物数据库和知识库的策展，但其应用受到多种挑战和障碍的阻碍，包括智能体的访问问题和适当的培训不足。作者希望通过云端环境和培训工作坊来缓解这些障碍。

Method: 基于JupyterHub平台构建云端智能体辅助策展环境，使用Claude Code作为通用工具框架（universal harness），通过浏览器终端与智能体会话交互，并通过单一API网关集中访问。创建了四个培训模块，从基础智能体工具使用逐步过渡到使用GO-CAM工具进行智能体辅助的生物通路策展。组织37名参与者参加四小时工作坊。

Result: 37名参与者参加了为期四小时的工作坊并完成了培训。关键发现是建立社区智能体AI能力主要是一个关于访问、工作流设计和培训的问题，而非技术本身。

Conclusion: 通过消除技术障碍、循序渐进地引入能力、将练习扎根于熟悉的策展任务，以及让策展人直接体验评估智能体输出，可以为在分布式科学社区中建立共享的智能体AI能力提供一条切实可行的路径。

Abstract: Agentic AI has the potential to accelerate curation of biological databases and knowledge bases. However, uptake has been hindered by a number of challenges and obstacles, including access to agents and appropriate training. Here we describe how we have attempted to address and mitigate these challenges and obstacles through the deployment of a cloud-based agentic environment, and the development of an interactive training workshop for the Gene Ontology Consortium. Our cloud environment for agentic-assisted curation was based on the JupyterHub platform, and utilized Claude Code as a universal harness. This allows curators to interact with an agent session through a terminal running in the browser, and has additional benefits such as centralization of access through a single API gateway, removing the need for participants to manage subscriptions or install software locally. We created four training modules, walking participants through basic agentic tool use first and then working up to agentic biological pathway curation using the existing GO-CAM (GO Causal Activity Model) curation tool. Thirty-seven participants took part in the four-hour workshop. Our key takeaway from this workshop is that building community capability with agentic AI is primarily a problem of access, workflow design, and training. Removing technical barriers, introducing capabilities gradually, grounding exercises in familiar curation tasks, and giving curators direct experience evaluating agent output can provide a practical route toward building shared agentic AI capability in distributed scientific communities.

</details>


### [154] [PCFBench: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Product Carbon Footprint Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27716)
*Krishna Rao,Andrew Dumit,Shaena Ulissi,Jacob Feintzeig,P. James Joyce,Daniel Frank,Steven Watson,Jonathan Glidden,Gizem Ilayda Dinc,Travis M. Kwee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PCFBench，首个将产品碳足迹（PCF）建模拆解为六个可独立评估子任务的基准（分解、检索、本体匹配、数值提取），包含614个专家标注项，涉及欠规范推理、冲突语境和数值约束。评测发现八种前沿LLM无一全面占优：最强模型估算总排放量在77%产品上达到2倍误差以内，但分步生成时正确率降至37-58%，仅45-75%满足质量守恒，暴露了AI代理在分步流程中的系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PCF评估要么只评分总排放量（掩盖错误来源、错误相互抵消），要么孤立地评估子任务（忽略组合交互）。高风险的PCF工作流要求每一步中间结果都正确，但缺乏能同时考察中间步骤正确性与组合效果的基准。

Method: 将PCF建模分解为六类可独立评估的任务：分解、检索、本体匹配、数值提取。构建614个专家标注的评测项，并设计能同时探测欠规范推理、冲突上下文和数值约束的测试场景。基于该数据集评估八种来自四家提供商的前沿LLM。

Result: 没有单一模型在所有任务上全面占优。最强模型在77%的产品上能估算总排放量且误差在2倍以内，但分步生成PCF时正确率降至37-58%，且只有45-75%的结果满足质量守恒约束，说明分步错误累积导致总体验证率明显下降。

Conclusion: 当前AI代理在分步PCF生成中缺乏透明度与可靠性，其失败会破坏从业者比较产品和推动脱碳所需的信任。作者公开发布数据集与评估工具，以支持针对性的模型改进。

Abstract: AI systems are being deployed on high-stakes, domain-specific workflows that demand correctness not just in the final output, but at every intermediate step. One such workflow is estimating a product carbon footprint (PCF), the greenhouse-gas emissions attributable to a physical product. AI agents are increasingly being used to generate PCFs, but existing evaluations score either total emissions (hiding error sources and cancelling mistakes) or sub-tasks in isolation (missing compositional interactions). We introduce PCFBench, the first benchmark to carve PCF modeling into independently-evaluable tasks that require decomposition, retrieval, ontology matching, and numerical extraction. It comprises 614 expert-labelled items across six tasks. Together they probe reasoning under under-specification, conflicting context, and numerical constraints. Across eight frontier LLMs from four providers, no single model dominates. Although the strongest models estimate total product emissions within 2 times of declared totals on 77% of products, this rate drops to 37-58% when the PCF is generated step by step, with only 45-75% obeying mass conservation. These failures undermine the transparency practitioners need to compare products and drive decarbonization. We release the dataset and evaluation harness to support targeted progress.

</details>


### [155] [Probing Perceptual Priors of MLLMs via Gibbs Sampling with Interpretable Generative Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27727)
*Manuel Cherep,Pattie Maes,Nikhil Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种直接采样模型"感知先验分布"的方法：通过控制生成模型的生成轴，并以目标模型为评判者运行吉布斯采样，从而在输入空间（如人脸可信度、艺术图像廉价感等）上恢复模型隐含的先验偏好，揭示了直接提示无法发现的既有偏差和新颖先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型行为由输入和先验共同决定，但现有可解释性研究要么固定输入进行机制分析（展示模型能表征什么而非期望什么），要么通过输入-输出变化进行行为分析（受限于固定刺激集，无法覆盖高维多样输入空间）。先验分布作为影响实际行为的组成部分仍未被充分理解。

Method: 提出一种采样方法：通过可控轴引导生成模型产生候选刺激，以目标模型作为评判者对这些刺激打分，并在该生成空间中运行吉布斯采样，从而直接从模型的感知先验分布中进行采样。

Result: 将该方法应用于多种类别和目标变量（如人脸可信度、艺术图像的廉价感），恢复了已知的典型偏差，也发现了直接提示无法显现的、令人惊讶的新颖先验。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效提取模型的感知先验，揭示直接提示不可见的隐藏偏见，这些新发现的先验值得进一步研究其对下游行为的影响。

Abstract: A model's behavior on a task is jointly determined by the input it receives and the prior it brings in, i.e. the distribution over stimuli it implicitly expects. Interpretability research has traditionally studied models by holding inputs fixed and examining model responses either mechanistically, probing how internal structure represents inputs, or behaviorally, measuring how variation in inputs leads to variation in outputs. Neither reconstructs the prior distribution itself, since internal structure shows what a model can represent, not what it expects, and any fixed stimulus set leaves most of the possible input space unseen. In particular, such an input space in real-world settings, such as images seen by VLMs, is extremely high-dimensional and diverse. These priors thus remain a poorly understood component of models that nonetheless influence real-world behavior. We propose a method to sample from models' perceptual prior distributions directly, by steering a generative model to produce stimuli along controllable axes and running Gibbs sampling over that space with the model under study as the judge. We apply this to a variety of categories and target variables (such as trustworthiness in faces and cheapness in art images) and recover both canonical biases and surprising novel priors invisible to direct prompting, warranting further investigation of their downstream effects.

</details>


### [156] [Why Didn't It Check? Unsupported Final Claims and Their Repair in Two Tool-Equipped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27768)
*Justin Bronder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型在已有工具可用且指令明确禁止猜测的情况下，仍会做出与证据不符的最终断言这一问题。作者将这一失败模式分解为两个可精确量化的指标：发生频率（模型自行做出无依据断言的频率）和条件修复率（提供缺失证据后，这些自然发生的无依据断言被修复的比例）。在固定的Qwen3-32B设置下，256个新提示模板中的512个首次回复里有33个以无依据的确立断言结尾；在精确复现的状态下重放，证据解析修复了33/33，无信息响应修复0/33。自动检查规则在64个需要证据的案例中增加了21次证据调用，纠正了全部10个错误的无依据断言，保留了11个偶然正确的断言，且从未将正确答案改为错误答案。在Gemma 4固定设置下，模型在所有512次首次回复中都调用了工具，未出现无依据的最终断言，因此无法测量条件修复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型即便在拥有工具访问权限、且指令明确禁止假设和猜测的情况下，仍可能做出最终断言，而这些断言与模型已见到的证据不符。即使单个可用的工具调用就能解决不确定性，模型也不一定会使用。作者希望精确量化这一失败模式，以理解其发生频率和可修复性。

Method: 将失败分解为两个指标：(1) occurrence——仅根据可见证据和最终声明衡量模型自行做出无依据断言的频率（不使用隐藏的正确回答）；(2) conditional repair——在提供缺失证据时，这些自然发生的无依据断言被修复的频率。实验从精确的副本状态重放每个案例，对比具有相同结构和长度、仅一个字符响应码不同的替代工具响应。同时设计了一个自动检查规则实验来评估证据补全的效果。

Result: 在Qwen3-32B设置下，512个首次回复中33个以无依据陈述结束；重放33次全部被证据修复，而携带无用信息的匹配响应修复0次。证据支持原始答案时33/33保持正确，未观察到损伤。64个需要证据的案例中，自动检查规则增加了21次证据调用，纠正全部10个错误的无依据声明，保留了11个偶然正确的声明，且从未将正确回答变为错误。Gemma 4设置下模型始终调用工具，无无依据声明，无法测量条件修复率。

Conclusion: 研究描述了两种固定本地模型设置在两个合成任务家族上的行为，证实了工具增强语言模型可能做出与可见证据矛盾的最终断言，且该失败在提供正确证据后几乎完全可修复。但结果不表明此失败在真实部署中的普遍性，也不代表跨模型共享的通用机制。

Abstract: A language model with access to tools can commit to a final claim unsupported by the evidence it has seen, even when a single available tool call would resolve the uncertainty and its instructions explicitly forbid assumptions and guesses. We separate this failure into two precisely defined quantities: occurrence, how often the model makes an unsupported claim on its own, measured from the visible evidence and final claim without using the hidden correct answer; and conditional repair, how often those same naturally occurring unsupported claims are repaired when the missing evidence is supplied. On one fixed Qwen3-32B setup, 33 of 512 first responses to 256 new prompt templates ended with an unsupported established claim. We replayed each case from an exact copy of the state in which the claim occurred; within each matched replay, the alternative tool responses had the same structure and length and differed only in a one-character response code. Resolving evidence repaired 33 of 33 claims; a matched response carrying no useful information repaired 0 of 33. When the evidence supported the original answer, the model preserved 33 of 33, with no observed harm. In a separate experiment, on 64 cases where evidence was needed, an automatic checking rule added 21 evidence calls, corrected all 10 wrong unsupported claims, preserved the 11 that were correct by accident, and never changed a correct answer into a wrong one. On a fixed Gemma 4 setup using the same sampling settings, the model called the tool in all 512 first responses and never made an unsupported final claim, so conditional repair could not be measured for that setup. These results describe two local fixed model setups on two synthetic task families. They do not show how common this failure is in real-world deployments, nor that it reflects a general mechanism shared across models.

</details>


### [157] [Credo: Reusable Declarative Primitives for Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27790)
*Duo Lu,Andrew Crotty,Uğur Çetintemel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Credo系统从搜索得到的LLM harness（命令式代码块）中恢复结构化的声明式描述，为每个提取的原始组件标注元数据并编目存储（带来源信息），使得编译器能够复用这些原始组件为新的任务生成harness，而无需从零开始重新搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM应用同时依赖模型和harness（决定每次调用的输入、调用次数和答案信任策略的程序）。编码智能体可以通过搜索找到强大的harness，但得到的产物是不可读的命令式代码块，其逻辑步骤、运行时信号、物理执行决策和提示策略都是隐式的且任务特定，导致后续任务必须从头开始搜索。然而，这些搜索得到的harness蕴含大量可复用的知识（有效的逻辑步骤、关键信号、自适应执行的物理操作决策和有效的提示策略），但都深埋在命令式代码中，缺乏可检查、可复用的结构，也没有来源信息或元数据。

Method: Credo通过三个核心步骤解决该问题：(1) 从搜索得到的harness中恢复结构化的声明式描述；(2) 为每个提取的原始组件（primitive）标注相关元数据；(3) 将所有内容带来源信息（provenance）编目存储。随后通过编译器将存储的原始组件绑定起来，为新的任务生成harness，避免重新启动搜索过程。

Result: 论文提供了初步结果，展示了该方法的潜力。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一个数据库社区非常适合的研究议程，包括：声明式目录上的基于成本的编译（cost-based compilation over declarative catalogs）、以及在模型和工作负载漂移下的目录维护（catalog maintenance under model and workload drift）。

Abstract: An LLM application depends on both a model and a harness: the program that determines what each call sees, how many calls to make, and which answers to trust. Coding agents can now discover strong harnesses by searching over candidate programs, but the resulting artifact is an opaque block of imperative code whose logical steps, runtime signals, physical execution decisions, and prompt strategies remain implicit and task-specific, forcing subsequent tasks to start the harness search process from scratch. The potential for reuse, however, is substantial. A searched harness encodes significant knowledge, such as the logical steps that work, the signals that matter, the physical operator decisions that adapt execution, and the prompt strategies that are effective, yet this knowledge is buried in imperative code with no inspectable or reusable structure, nor does it carry any provenance or metadata. Credo addresses this problem by recovering a structured declarative description of a searched harness, tagging each extracted primitive with relevant metadata, and cataloguing all of it with provenance. A compiler can then bind stored primitives to generate harnesses for new tasks without having to start the search over from scratch. This paper provides preliminary results demonstrating the potential of our approach and lays out a related research agenda that the database community is well-positioned to tackle, including cost-based compilation over declarative catalogs and catalog maintenance under model and workload drift.

</details>


### [158] [ReToolSQL: Agentic Reinforcement Learning for Robust Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27796)
*Pratik Kakkar,Chandra Dhir,Ravi Shankar,Pareekshit Reddy Gaddam,Anup Shirgaonkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReToolSQL，一种两阶段训练框架用于文本到SQL生成：先对拒绝采样的推理轨迹进行监督预训练（SFT），再对多轮工具使用轨迹进行代理强化微调（RFT）。在BIRD-SQL开发集上达到74.32%的单次执行准确率（self-consistency下74.77%），排名第一，全部基于单个31B模型实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到SQL的强化学习方法大多将SQL生成视为单轮任务，限制了模型通过迭代纠错从执行反馈中恢复错误的能力。需要一种能够利用多轮工具使用和执行反馈进行迭代精化的训练框架。

Method: ReToolSQL包含两个互补阶段：(1) 监督式冷启动，在拒绝采样（rejection-sampled）的推理轨迹上进行SFT，扩展可解问题集合并提升pass@k覆盖率；(2) 代理强化微调（RFT），在多轮工具使用轨迹上训练，教模型何时验证、检索什么证据以及如何从执行反馈中修复错误的SQL。奖励基于执行正确性的复合信号，无需人工标注。

Result: 在Gemma 4 instruction-tuned (31B)模型上，RFT单独达到73.66% EX（self-consistency下74.12%），SFT→RFT达到74.32%单次EX和74.77% self-consistency EX，在BIRD单模型开发集排行榜上排名第一。

Conclusion: 设计良好的SFT→RFT流水线配合工具使用轨迹是通向企业级文本到SQL的实用路径。两个阶段在互补维度上起作用：SFT扩展可解问题集，RFT将扩展的能力转化为更高单次准确率，且整个过程无需额外人工标注，仅在单个31B密集模型中实现。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that reinforcement learning from execution feedback can substantially improve text-to-SQL performance, often enabling smaller models to match or exceed much larger systems. However, most existing approaches treat SQL generation as a single-turn task, limiting the model's ability to recover from errors through iterative refinement.
